You are here when: The large sandy region containing an oasis, buried ruins, broken highways, and a solar tower.

Photo Challenges

You are here when: Painted silhouettes hidden on walls and objects in the city, Wasteland, and Great Desert.

The four board requests contain one tutorial image followed by three ten-image regional sets.

Within the Memories (missable)

Photo target

HintUse the Data Bank thumbnail as a gentle visual clue and scan walls without leaving the current region.
WhereIn the active photo-challenge region, follow this compact landmark chain: Xion → Memory → Square → central → sphere.
Full detailAt the end of that landmark chain, frame the painted target behind EVE in Selfie Mode and wait for the scan confirmation. If the route is still closed, satisfy this prerequisite chain first: Photography → Club → request → active. Open Selfie Mode with EVE facing the camera, keep the painted target clearly behind her, adjust until the recognition frame appears, then take the picture and confirm its Data Bank slot fills.

Nano Suit Catalogue

You are here when: The Exterior menu, design-pattern crates, affinity shops, challenge rewards, and installed-content grants.

The current patched PC catalogue contains 126 Nano Suits. Later-cycle crates only advance when the earlier design from that location was already collected.

HintKeep opening combat and supply rewards; this family has multiple strength grades.

Skills and Permanent Upgrades

You are here when: Camp skill menus, repair consoles, the drone interface, shops, and the Equipment screen.

All base skills

Progression goal

HintSpend earned points across every skill tab.
WhereUse camps to inspect Attack, Survival, Beta, Burst, and Tachy branches for empty nodes.
Full detailFarm remaining skill points before the regional lock, buy every visible node in every base tree, and verify no purchasable node remains.

All New Game Plus skills

Progression goal

HintA completed save opens additional combat upgrades.
WhereStart New Game Plus and inspect the expanded Beta and Burst branches at a camp.
Full detailCarry the completed save into New Game Plus, earn enough points for every newly added Beta and Burst upgrade, and verify the Infinite Blade completion record in the system menu.

Maximum Body Core upgrades

Progression goal

HintKeep inspecting fallen Airborne bodies in every region.
WhereCollect Body Cores until the health meter reaches its first-run cap, then continue the added tiers in New Game Plus.
Full detailUse every regional Body Core entry, then repeat core pickups in New Game Plus until the extended health cap stops increasing.

Maximum Beta Core upgrades

Progression goal

HintKeep inspecting fallen Airborne bodies in every region.
WhereCollect Beta Cores until the energy meter reaches its first-run cap, then continue the added tiers in New Game Plus.
Full detailUse every regional Beta Core entry, then repeat core pickups in New Game Plus until the extended Beta-energy cap stops increasing.

Blood Edge level 40

Progression goal

HintEvery major combat reward contributes to the weapon's final ceiling.
WhereCollect all normal and damaged Weapon Cores across successive cycles.
Full detailClaim every regional core and exchange damaged sets when available; continue through New Game Plus until the weapon status displays level 40.

Rechargeable Tumbler Enhancement II level 8

Progression goal

HintSmall carrier enemies hide the healing-capacity modules.
WhereCollect every Tumbler Expansion Module in the first run and the additional later-cycle modules.
Full detailDefeat each module carrier in the regional checklist, then repeat the applicable pickups in New Game Plus until the tumbler shows Enhancement II level 8.

All drone upgrades

Progression goal

HintDestroy every small module carrier and revisit the upgrade console.
WhereCollect all Drone Upgrade Modules and purchase every ranged-mode node.
Full detailUse the regional module entries, then spend them at the drone upgrade interface until every ammunition and firing-mode node is filled, including later-cycle additions.

All Exospine socket expansions

Progression goal

HintThe plain supply boxes also hide the bolts needed for equipment capacity.
WhereOpen every box and spend each complete Omnibolt set at a repair console.
Full detailDo not stop at the minimum expansion: clear all 299 base boxes, collect every Omnibolt they contain, and purchase every available Exospine socket expansion.

Maximum affinity with every shop

Progression goal

HintBuy ordinary stock before expecting each vendor's hidden inventory.
WhereRaise Roxanne, Lyle, Kaya, D1G-g2r, Clyde, and other affinity meters to their maximum tier.
Full detailPurchase inexpensive repeatable stock until each affinity tier advances, revisit after regional returns, then buy every newly revealed record, design, accessory, companion outfit, drone appearance, bait, and music item.

All non-suit cosmetics and music

Progression goal

HintCheck every Exterior submenu and camp music list after updates and DLC tasks.
WhereComplete the glasses, earrings, hairstyles, companion outfits, drone appearances, accessories, and camp-track catalogues.
Full detailMax every shop, enable both seasonal themes, clear all photo and collaboration tasks, claim the seven arranged tracks at the hub bar in New Game Plus, and verify no locked slot remains in any Exterior or music submenu.

Boss Challenge

You are here when: The separate challenge option populated by battle data from completed encounters.

Normal clears provide the mode rewards; Hard is included because it is a distinct supported completion tier.

Base Boss Challenge #01

Boss Challenge entry

HintFinish the corresponding campaign encounter to add its battle data.
WhereSelect the newly registered entry from Boss Challenge after completing the campaign.
Full detailClear this entry on Normal, then repeat it on Hard with either personal equipment or a preset; verify both results in the challenge record.

Endings, Later Cycles, and Secrets

You are here when: The final camp, cleared-save continuation, seasonal hub and oasis variants, and the sealed Matrix 11 room.

The outfit catalogue requires New Game, New Game Plus, and New Game Plus Plus reward passes even when ending backups reduce repeated finale runs.

Ending path A (missable)

Ending

HintOne final response leads to a distinct conclusion.
WhereAt the final decision, choose the first response after entering with a fully completed companion-progress gauge.
Full detailBack up at the final camp. With the optional-progress gauge full, choose the affirmative response, finish the remaining encounters, watch the complete ending and its added epilogue, then restore the backup.

Ending path B (missable)

Ending

HintThe same final response changes when optional progress is deliberately limited.
WhereReach the final decision on a later cycle without filling the companion-progress gauge, then choose the first response.
Full detailUse a New Game Plus speed run that avoids optional records and quests which raise the companion gauge. At the final camp make a backup, choose the affirmative response, and finish the resulting ending.

Ending path C (missable)

Ending

HintThe alternative final response leads to the remaining conclusion.
WhereAt the final decision, choose the second response; gauge state does not gate this path.
Full detailRestore the final-camp backup from either route, choose the negative response, complete the remaining encounter, and watch the ending through its post-credits return.

Three added epilogues (missable)

Post-game scene

HintA current patch adds short follow-up scenes after a qualifying conclusion.
WhereComplete the fully prepared affirmative ending on the current patch, then revisit the hub interactions made available afterward.
Full detailAfter viewing the qualifying ending on patch 1.4.1 or later, continue the cleared save and inspect the newly available hub follow-ups until all three epilogue vignettes have played.

Hard-mode campaign clear

Difficulty clear

HintThe highest campaign setting is a separate mastery route.
WhereSelect Hard for a carried-over cycle after finishing the first campaign.
Full detailStart New Game Plus on Hard, retain your equipment, complete the full campaign without lowering the setting, and keep the cleared Hard save for Boss Challenge cleanup.

Summer Oasis interactions

Seasonal activity

HintA settings toggle keeps the summer content available outside its original season.
WhereEnable the summer theme, visit the Great Desert oasis, use the added interaction, and clear Clyde's seasonal stock.
Full detailSet Seasonal Content to On, travel to the oasis, interact with the sunbed, buy both summer suits and the accessory from Clyde, and confirm the alternate camp tracks are selectable.

Holiday city minigame and rewards

Seasonal activity

HintA settings toggle keeps the holiday content available outside its original season.
WhereEnable the holiday theme and inspect the decorated city hub for its minigame and shop rewards.
Full detailSet Seasonal Content to On, clear the holiday target minigame, then claim Santa Dress, the companion and drone outfits, the hairstyle, glasses, both earrings, and both seasonal music tracks.

Sealed studio room (missable)

Secret

HintA late hidden request finally opens one previously sealed room.
WhereComplete Strange Signal, then revisit the Rotten Labyrinth route in Matrix 11.
Full detailFast travel to Temporary Armoury Entrance, ride the nearby lift, follow the right-hand route past the enemies, open the sealed door with θεθλβλ, inspect the full studio room, and take Ocean String.
